## Semantic Chunks
### Section: Introduction
- **Level**: `intro`
- **Hash**: `042e77e48f74ede5a4cb29a76e9f6f19d9e2f7278907af1a43cb18d072d7e03e`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-writing-history-reveal-humanized-ai-content` (Similarity: 0.9124)

```text
Yes. Typing history provides the definitive behavioral proof of whether an essay was genuinely composed by a human student or assembled through automated shortcuts.

While statistical AI detectors generate probability scores that can fluctuate, typing history records the physical reality of creation. An authentic essay leaves an unmistakable developmental footprint: brainstorming notes, multi-day drafting sessions, frequent backspaces, clause deletions, paragraph reorganizations, and natural thinking pauses. A synthetic or copied essay, by contrast, appears as an instant wholesale paste or an unbroken linear transcription devoid of human struggle.

Through Checkmark Plagiarism's Writing Process Analysis , educators can verify authentic student authorship with absolute objective clarity.

Checkmark Plagiarism powers authenticity analysis by pairing essay writing playback with AI detection , plagiarism detection , autograding , and integrations with Canvas and Google Classroom .
```

### Section: The 4 Hallmarks of Authentic Typing History
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `aa8feb1f2e3c003b49ff3697408d5d3a764fcf4c2a55d4e0c84acb74275908cd`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-can-department-chairs-standardize-rubric-grading-across-multiple-class-sections-in-canvas-lms` (Similarity: 0.8410)

```text
1. Multi-Day Session Continuity

Authentic essays develop across 3–6 distinct sessions over multiple calendar dates, proving steady intellectual engagement over time.

2. High Revision & Backspace Depth

Authentic student writers constantly refine their words, exhibiting a 15–30% backspace rate and active restructuring of sentences.

3. Non-Linear Document Evolution

Writers jump between sections: drafting an introduction, adding evidence to paragraph 2, jumping to the conclusion, and formatting citations.

4. Proportional Active Typing Time

A 1,500-word essay requires 3 to 5 hours of active keyboard engagement, matching normal human typing and ideation speeds.
```

### Section: What Inauthentic Writing History Looks Like
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `f0b21047c07f2993c103c4f808625c8528cdcfc233b69edbb17d7473208679f9`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-ai-paraphrasing-tools-beat-plagiarism-checkers` (Similarity: 0.8528)

```text
In contrast to authentic drafting, artificial shortcuts leave unmistakable timeline anomalies:

The Instant Paste: An empty document suddenly gains 1,200 words at 11:30 PM in a single millisecond with 0 minutes of active typing. 
 The Robotic Waterfall: The paper is typed linearly from the first word to the last word with 
 The Export-and-Replace: A rough draft is deleted and replaced by a polished text block in one paste event after being rewritten externally.

Read more in how Checkmark writing process analysis works .
```

### Section: Comparison: Authentic Drafting History vs. AI Shortcut History
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `ecdccf0f6c651d50c2638bd5a91ee3bfe298cf0e0383d2596a7d0d920c07282e`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/what-does-an-ai-written-essay-look-like-in-writing-history` (Similarity: 0.9498)

```text
Authentic Writing History (Verified Human)

4 sessions across 8 days (3.8 hours active typing). 
 22% backspaces and deletions (heavy editing). 
 Non-linear drafting flow and paragraph moves. 
 Citations added incrementally over multiple days.

Inauthentic Writing History (AI Shortcut)

1 session lasting 4 minutes total. 

 1,400 words inserted via external clipboard paste. 
 Citations pasted simultaneously with body text.
```

### Section: A 5-Step Educator Protocol for Verifying Authenticity
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `fcf6eefb534ca33d9333bc86729bd557f4cdf8419ec68d83b0f952fba6b91019`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-do-i-find-out-whether-a-student-actually-wrote-an-assignment` (Similarity: 0.8799)

```text
Educator Authenticity Verification Protocol:

1. Open the Checkmark Playback summary in Canvas SpeedGrader or Google Classroom. 
 2. Review the session breakdown to verify multi-day drafting distribution. 
 3. Confirm total active typing hours match the expected scope of the paper. 
 4. Check the backspace and revision rate: authentic drafting exceeds 15%. 
 5. Verify that flagged paste blocks correspond to properly cited quotations.
```
